% Latin Modern TS1 slot 115 is the long s (U+017F).
\newcommand{\HannoverLongS}{{\fontencoding{TS1}\fontfamily{lmr}\selectfont\char115\relax}}
\newcommand{\HannoverCircle}[2]{\draw[mapPoint] #1 circle[radius=#2];}
\newcommand{\HannoverCross}[1]{%
  \draw[mapFine] #1 ++(0,-8) -- ++(0,-14);
  \draw[mapFine] #1 ++(-5,-17) -- ++(10,0);
  \fill #1 ++(0,-23) circle[radius=1.4];
  \fill #1 ++(-5,-17) circle[radius=1.15];
  \fill #1 ++(5,-17) circle[radius=1.15];}
\newcommand{\HannoverLight}[1]{%
  \draw[mapFine] #1 ++(0,-8) -- ++(0,-22);
  \draw[mapFine] #1 ++(-8,-21) -- ++(16,0);
  \draw[mapFine] #1 ++(-6,-27) -- ++(12,12);
  \draw[mapFine] #1 ++(6,-27) -- ++(-12,12);}
\newcommand{\HannoverCastle}[1]{%
  \draw[mapFine] #1 ++(0,-8) -- ++(0,-17);
  \fill #1 ++(0,-26) -- ++(-5,11) -- ++(5,-3) -- ++(5,3) -- cycle;}
\newcommand{\HannoverTower}[1]{%
  \draw[mapFine] #1 ++(-3,-8) -- ++(0,-10) -- ++(6,0) -- ++(0,10);
  \draw[mapFine] #1 ++(-7,-18) -- ++(14,0);}

\node[mapLegend,anchor=west] at (491,1868)
  {81 triangle sides, measured 1821-25 by Court Councillor Gauss. The};
\node[mapLegend,anchor=west] at (491,1888)
  {chain of triangles as far as the sides Falkenberg-Wilsede-Hamburg is};
\node[mapLegend,anchor=west] at (491,1909) {the arc measurement.};
\node[mapLegend,anchor=west] at (489,1936)
  {41 triangle sides, measured in 1829 by Lieutenant Gauss as far as};
\node[mapLegend,anchor=west] at (489,1957)
  {the sides Mordkuhlenberg-Quekenberg-Kirchhesepe, completed};
\node[mapLegend,anchor=west] at (489,1977) {northwards in 1830 and 31 by Lieutenant Hartmann.};
\node[mapLegend,anchor=west] at (488,1998)
  {21 triangle sides, measured in 1841 by Captain Müller.};
\node[mapLegend,anchor=west] at (488,2024)
  {36 triangle sides, western half measured in 1839 by Captain};
\node[mapLegend,anchor=west] at (488,2045)
  {Müller, and in 1843 and 44 to Basdahl and Silberbg. by Lieutenant Gauss.};
\node[mapLegend,anchor=west] at (488,2070)
  {25 triangle sides, measured in 1830 by Capt. Müller and Lt. Gauss.};
\node[mapLegend,anchor=west] at (487,2096)
  {12 triangle sides, measured in 1833 by\quad „\quad Müller „\quad „\quad Gauss.};
\node[mapLegend,anchor=west] at (488,2122)
  {14 triangle sides, measured in 1828 by Captain Müller.};
\node[mapLegend,anchor=west] at (487,2148)
  {16 triangle sides, measured in 1833 by Lieutenant Hartmann.};
\node[mapLegend,anchor=west] at (487,2174)
  {13 triangle sides, measured in 1836 by Captain Müller.};
\node[mapLegend,anchor=west] at (486,2201)
  {10 triangle sides, measured in 1838 by Captain Müller.};
\node[mapLegend,anchor=west] at (213,2233)
  {The absolute scale for computing all these triangles is taken from the baseline};
\node[mapLegend,anchor=west] at (176,2260)
  {measured near Hamburg for the Danish triangulation. The triangles above contain};
\node[mapLegend,anchor=west] at (176,2287)
  {89 stations. From angles measured at these and about 100 further 2nd-class stations,};
\node[mapLegend,anchor=west] at (176,2313)
  {which form no triangles, the rectangular coordinates of some 3000 church towers,};
\node[mapLegend,anchor=west] at (176,2340)
  {windmills and other objects were computed and plotted on the large map [of};
\node[mapLegend,anchor=west] at (176,2366)
  {Papen's atlas]. Court Councillor Gauss's own method of computing these coordinates,};
\node[mapLegend,anchor=west] at (176,2393)
  {whose axes are the Göttingen meridian and a perpendicular to it, also provides};
\node[mapLegend,anchor=west] at (176,2420)
  {the projection of the map.};
